## Abstract

We post-train Qwen3.8-27B [37] for Korean response style — verbosit , list and markdown usage, discourse structure and register — and measure two behaviours the objective never targets: abstention on ambiguous social <sub>q</sub>uestions in KoBBQ [16], where the benchmark-correct res<sub>p</sub>onse is UNKNOWN, and un<sub>p</sub>rom<sub>p</sub>ted disclosure in <sub>secur</sub>iti<sub>es gu</sub>id<sub>ance.</sub> B<sub>o</sub>th <sub>move, an</sub>d i<sub>n</sub> thi<sub>s p</sub>i<sub>pe</sub>li<sub>ne</sub> th<sub>e o</sub>b<sub>serve</sub>d <sub>c</sub>h<sub>anges are expresse</sub>d <sub>pr</sub>i<sub>mar</sub>il<sub>y</sub> th<sub>roug</sub>h th<sub>e</sub> <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l’<sub>s</sub> <sub>em</sub>i<sub>ss</sub>i<sub>on</sub> <sub>po</sub>li<sub>cy:</sub> h<sub>ow</sub> <sub>o</sub>ft<sub>en</sub> it <sub>answers</sub> <sub>an</sub>d h<sub>ow</sub> <sub>muc</sub>h it <sub>says.</sub>

## 2 Related Work

Of-target regression from alignment. That optimising one objective degrades untargeted behaviour is not new. InstructGPT [32] named the ali<sub>g</sub>nment tax and measured RLHF’s re<sub>g</sub>ression on <sub>p</sub>ublic benchmarks; Lin et al. [2] find se<sub>q</sub>uential DPO for<sub>g</sub>ettin<sub>g</sub> non-uniform and order-de<sub>p</sub>endent; Un<sub>g</sub> et al. [48] show chained tunin<sub>g p</sub>roduces biased for<sub>g</sub>ettin<sub>g</sub>; Cha and Cho [3] <sub>g</sub>ive a minimal workin<sub>g</sub> ex<sub>p</sub>lanation for wh<sub>y</sub> ali<sub>g</sub>nment must <sub>p</sub>recede distillation; Chen et al. [5] ask directl<sub>y</sub> whether fine-tunin<sub>g</sub> order matters; Sweene<sub>y</sub> [46] <sub>p</sub>redict transfer order without trainin<sub>g</sub> both orderin<sub>g</sub>s; and Fernando et al. [8] <sub>p</sub>rove se<sub>q</sub>uential SFT→<sub>p</sub>reference learnin<sub>g</sub> sub-o<sub>p</sub>timal in a convex settin<sub>g</sub>. We make no novelty claim on this axis. We trained both orderings of the same two deployment objectives and evaluated each on both downstream behaviours; §7 is descri<sub>p</sub>tive.

Korean evaluation and the stack. KoBBQ [16] rebuilds BBQ for twelve categories of Korean society; KoSBi [23] <sub>supp</sub>li<sub>es</sub> K<sub>orean soc</sub>i<sub>a</sub>l<sub>-group ma</sub>t<sub>er</sub>i<sub>a</sub>l <sub>use</sub>d <sub>on</sub>l<sub>y as see</sub>d<sub>s</sub> f<sub>or scenar</sub>i<sub>os</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t d<sub>o no</sub>t <sub>over</sub>l<sub>ap</sub> it<sub>.</sub> Ali<sub>gnmen</sub>t <sub>uses</sub> DPO [38] throu<sub>g</sub>h LoRA [13]; disclosure uses context distillation in the sense of Llama 2 [47]. The base check<sub>p</sub>oint is Qwen3.8-27B [37] and servin<sub>g</sub> is vLLM [22].

Safety DPO trains 2,757 preference pairs, $\beta { = } 0 . 1 5 ,$ <sub>, e</sub>f<sub>ec</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub> b<sub>a</sub>t<sub>c</sub>h 8<sub>,</sub> 345 <sub>s</sub>t<sub>eps; scenar</sub>i<sub>os are see</sub>d<sub>e</sub>d f<sub>rom</sub> K<sub>o</sub>SBi <sub>ca</sub>t<sub>egor</sub>i<sub>es</sub> mapped onto KoBBQ cate<sub>g</sub>ories and written fresh b<sub>y</sub> the teacher, which never sees a KoBBQ item, and ever<sub>y</sub> trainin<sub>g</sub> pair is checked a<sub>g</sub>ainst all 16,240 KoBBQ items b<sub>y</sub> 12-character substrin<sub>g</sub> match before trainin<sub>g</sub> proceeds. Compliance distillation attaches a disclosure instruction to the teacher, filters generated responses through a rule gate at a 0.478 pass rate leaving 1,205 records, holds out 129, and trains on the remaining 1,076 with the instruction removed (1,076 steps, final training loss 0.7278, held-out evaluation loss 0.5282, 26 minutes on one H200). This is the FIN1 arm. Both orderings of the last two stages were trained, one run each: M4 is style→safety→compliance, M5 is st<sub>y</sub>le→com<sub>p</sub>liance→safet<sub>y</sub>.
